### sweep-science


#### [blocker] SCI-01 — Herzog metabolic model outputs metabolic cost but is labelled, styled and exported as travel time in minutes

- 위치: `tools/cost_surface_dialog.py` :2570 (layer name); 2076-2085 (lblModelHelp); 2856-2857 (units="min"); 2624 (등시간선 layer); 3479, 3506 (LCP profile summary); cost_surface_dialog_base.ui:126; tools/cost_models.py:135-155
- 문제: The 6th-order polynomial is Minetti et al. (2002) metabolic cost of walking in J·kg⁻¹·m⁻¹ (den = 1.64 at zero slope, the standard flat-ground value), which Herzog (2013) uses as a COST, not as a speed. The code converts it to a speed via v = v0 · 1.64/den, i.e. it silently assumes the walker holds metabolic POWER constant and lets speed fall in inverse proportion to cost per metre. That assumption appears nowhere: not in lblModelHelp (2076-2085), not in the tool help (1906-1946), not in REFERENCES.md's Herzog entry, which documents only the abs-slope isotropy difference. Everything downstream then presents the number as a clock time: the layer is named 누적 비용(분) (Cumulative Cost, min), the metadata carries units="min", the contour layer is literally 등시간선 (Isochrones) with 15/30/45/60-minute …
- 실패 시나리오: A user maps a 5 km route on a sustained 20% grade with 허조그 메타볼릭 selected and 누적 비용 래스터 + 등시간선 ticked. Measured by running the repo's own tools/cost_models.edge_cost over a 100 m horizontal edge: Herzog 354.0 s, Tobler 143.9 s, Naismith 192.0 s (at 30% grade: 498.0 / 204.2 / 252.0 s; all three agree to within 1% at zero slope, 72.0 vs 71.5 s). Herzog therefore reports the 5 km as 295 minutes (4.9 h) where Tobler reports 120 minutes (2.0 h) and Naismith 160 minutes (2.7 h) — an implied walking speed of 1.02 km/h up an 11° slope. The map shows a layer called '누적 비용(분) (Cumulative Cost, min) - 허조그 …
- 제안 수정: Add one sentence to lblModelHelp at cost_surface_dialog.py:2076-2085 and the same to the cost-network model help: "이 다항식은 Minetti 등(2002)의 경사별 대사 비용(J·kg⁻¹·m⁻¹)입니다. 본 도구는 '일정 대사출력' 가정으로 속도 = 기본속도 × 1.64/비용 으로 환산하므로, 출력 '분'은 에너지에 비례하는 상대 비용이지 검증된 보행 소요시간이 아닙니다(20% 오르막에서 Tobler의 약 2.4배). 실제 소요시간이 필요하면 Tobler 또는 Naismith를 쓰세요." Then stop calling it minutes for this model: name the layer 누적 비용(상대, 에너지비례) and set units="relative_cost" at 2856 so the align/export and AI-summary paths do not propagate …

#### [major] SCI-02 — Network interpretation guides present degree/betweenness as revealing 중심지·요충지, while k — the parameter that determines them — is documented only as a speed and de-cluttering knob

- 위치: `tools/spatial_network_dialog.py` :736-748 (SNA section); 706-710 (k presented as a 스파게티 knob); 317 (k tooltip); tools/cost_network_dialog.py:1985-1993 (SNA section), 1977 (k as accuracy knob); README.md line '후보 간선(k) 값을 키워 다시 실행하세요'
- 문제: A k-NN (or Gabriel/RNG/threshold) graph is constructed, not observed. Its degree and betweenness are functions of k and of the spatial point pattern, so they are properties of the analyst's parameter choice, not of past interaction. Both guides state the values depend on the graph TYPE (cost_network_dialog.py:1986 says '선택한 네트워크 방식에 따라 달라집니다'); neither says they depend on k, and the spatial-network guide has no dependence statement at all. Worse, the only places k is explained frame it as a convenience: spatial_network 706-710 lists it under '스파게티(선 과다) 줄이는 팁', its tooltip at 317 recommends 3~5 to avoid visual clutter, and cost_network 1977 plus the README present raising k as improving accuracy — i.e. the user is told to tune the answer-determining parameter for legibility. Nor does eithe …
- 실패 시나리오: Eight sites in a valley (coordinates in metres: A 0,0; B 900,200; C 1700,100; D 2600,400; E 1500,1400; F 2400,1600; G 3300,900; H 4200,1200), union k-NN, unweighted SNA. Driving the repo's own tools/network_metrics.betweenness_centrality_unweighted: at k=2 the result is C=11.5, D=6.5, E=3.0, G=2.5, F=1.5, A=B=H=0.0 — C is the unique broker, with 1.8x the next site, and the author writes 'C는 계곡 통행의 요충지로, 인접 유적 사이를 중개하는 결절점이다'. At k=3, the same eight coordinates give C=D=E=F=4.5 (a four-way tie) and G collapses from 2.5 to 0.0. Nothing changed but a spinbox whose tooltip told the user to pick 3~ …
- 제안 수정: Add one sentence to the SNA section of both guides (spatial_network_dialog.py:736 and cost_network_dialog.py:1985), in the Korean and English branches: "이 지표는 관측된 관계가 아니라 이 도구가 만든 그래프의 성질입니다. 값은 후보 간선 수(k)·그래프 규칙·입력한 유적 집합에 따라 달라지므로, 보고할 때는 k와 그래프 규칙을 함께 밝히고 최소 두 개의 k에서 순위가 유지되는지 확인하십시오. 누락된(미발견·멸실·다른 시기) 유적은 모든 값을 바꿉니다 (Brughmans & Peeples 2017; Amati et al. 2018)." Also amend the k tooltip at spatial_network_dialog.py:317 so it no longer reads as a purely cosmetic setting.

#### [major] SCI-03 — "Minetti(1995) - 에너지효율" slope preset claims an energy analysis it never computes, and its legend labels invert the paper's own result

- 위치: `tools/terrain_analysis_dialog_base.ui` :148-153 (radioMinetti label + tooltip); 138-143 (radioTobler tooltip); tools/terrain_analysis_dialog.py:91-100 (minetti class table), 399-404 (gdal:slope + layer name)
- 문제: Selecting this preset runs gdal:slope and applies a colour ramp — no energy quantity is computed anywhere in the file. The tooltip nevertheless prints the DOI and asserts the tool '경사에 따른 에너지 소비량(J/kg/m) 변화를 분석합니다' and supports '고대 노동력 추정'. Beyond the missing computation, the class table contradicts the cited paper. Minetti (1995) works in PERCENT gradient and its single published result is that the most economical mountain-path gradient is ≈25% (≈14°); it publishes no classification and no degree breaks. The plugin's breaks are degrees, so the gradient the paper identifies as OPTIMAL (14°) falls in the band labelled '고강도', and the band labelled '금지' begins at 25° — the paper's number with the unit silently swapped. REFERENCES.md:98 does mark this (C) and REFERENCES.md:58 does say the pres …
- 실패 시나리오: A user preparing a figure on quarry-to-site haulage picks 'Minetti(1995) - 에너지효율', reads the tooltip with its DOI, and produces a layer named '경사도_Minetti 1995' whose legend reads 일상 / 노동 / 고강도 / 임계 / 금지. The caption written from it is 'Minetti(1995)의 에너지 소비 함수 기준, 25° 이상 구간은 운반 금지 구간으로 분류된다' and the text adds that 9-15° ground was '고강도' haulage. A reviewer opens JAP 79(5):1698-1703: it contains no classification, no degree breaks, works in percent throughout, and its one substantive finding is that ≈25% (≈14°) is the MOST economical gradient — i.e. the plugin labels the paper's optimum as 'hi …
- 제안 수정: Give the Minetti tooltip the same treatment the Llobera tooltip at terrain_analysis_dialog_base.ui:163 already received: rename the radio to '경사 5등급(플러그인 자체 표)', and replace the tooltip body with "<b>주의:</b> 아래 0-3 / 3-9 / 9-15 / 15-25 / 25°+ 구간과 일상·노동·고강도·임계·금지 라벨은 이 플러그인이 정한 표시 구분입니다. Minetti (1995)는 분류표를 제시하지 않으며, 백분율 경사로 작업해 가장 경제적인 등판 경사를 약 25%(≈14°)로 보고합니다 — 본 표의 '25°' 구간과 혼동하지 마십시오. 이 도구는 에너지를 계산하지 않고 경사(°) 래스터에 색상만 입힙니다." Drop '고대 노동력 추정' from 고고학 활용, and apply the same qualification to …

#### [major] SCI-04 — Cumulative viewshed help says the count expresses 중요도, with nothing anywhere saying the count is a property of the observer sample and no null-model comparison offered

- 위치: `tools/viewshed_dialog.py` :354 (help); 366-372 (주의/팁 section); 4348 (legend labels '{k}개 누적')
- 문제: The raster is a count of how many of the analyst's chosen observer points see each cell — which the .ui tooltip at viewshed_dialog_base.ui:80 states accurately. The help then reframes that count as '중요도 표현', which is an inference the output cannot carry: the value at a cell is set by how many observers were selected and how densely they are packed, so it rises wherever the sample happens to clump and falls wherever the analyst did not include sites. The 주의/팁 section lists only DEM quality, observer/target height, curvature and layer clutter — it says nothing about the observer set conditioning the result, and the plugin offers no way to compare the surface against the same number of points placed at random on the same DEM, which is the standard control. Gillings & Wheatley (2001), already …
- 실패 시나리오: Twelve hilltop fort sites along one valley are run as a 누적 가시권 with '누적값을 개수로 표시' ticked. The legend reads 보이지 않음 / 1개 누적 / … / 12개 누적 and the raster peaks at 9 on a stretch of valley floor. Following the help's '중요도 표현', the author writes '9개 성곽에서 동시에 조망되는 이 구간은 감시의 초점이자 경관상 가장 중요한 지점이다'. The peak is in fact where the twelve chosen circles happen to overlap: adding four forts from the adjoining valley, or dropping the two whose identification is disputed, moves the peak several hundred metres, and 30 random points on the same DEM produce a comparable peak value. The paper has no basis for cal …
- 제안 수정: Change the bullet at viewshed_dialog.py:354 from '중요도 표현' to '몇 개의 관측점에서 보이는지 집계(중요도가 아님)', and add one bullet to 주의/팁 at 366-372: "누적값은 <b>사용자가 고른 관측점 집합의 성질</b>입니다. 관측점을 더하거나 빼면 최고값의 위치가 바뀌므로, 어떤 지점을 '유의하게 잘 보인다'고 주장하려면 같은 DEM 위에 같은 수의 무작위 점을 배치한 결과와 비교해야 합니다 (Gillings & Wheatley 2001). 결과를 보고할 때는 관측점의 수·출처·시기 동시성을 함께 밝히십시오."

#### [major] SCI-05 — Trench suggestion's proximity-to-known-sites score hard-wires the site-clustering assumption and can double-count it with the AHP raster the same plugin tells the user to build

- 위치: `tools/trench_suggestion_dialog.py` :1359-1362 (ref_score); 404, 419 (default weight 0.25, label 유적근접 가중치); 452 (help); 240-244 (header); docs/AHP_GUIDE.md:72 and tools/ahp_suitability_dialog.py:889 (the advice that creates the overlap)
- 문제: Two separate problems in one score. (1) ref_score rises linearly as a candidate approaches a known site, with a positive default weight of 0.25 and no option to invert it. That hard-wires the hypothesis that archaeological material clusters around already-known sites — and the known-site distribution is itself a record of where people have previously looked. Neither the help (452), the header (240-244), the weight label (419) nor the 유적 근접 점수 반경 spinbox carries any tooltip stating this. The only caveat offered is '매장문화재 존재를 보장하지 않습니다', which is about certainty, not about the direction of the assumption. (2) The same plugin's AHP help (ahp_suitability_dialog.py:889) and AHP_GUIDE.md:72 both explicitly recommend '유적까지 거리' as a cost criterion. A user who follows that advice feeds an AHP raste …
- 실패 시나리오: An AHP suitability raster is built following the guide's worked example (§9) with 유적까지 거리 substituted for 하천까지 거리 at the 29% weight the guide reports, then fed to the trench tool at its defaults (AHP 0.55, 유적근접 0.25, 경사 0.20, 반경 1000 m). Proximity-to-known-sites now drives 0.55×0.29 + 0.25 = 0.41 of every candidate's score — 64% more than the 0.25 the dialog shows and the metadata records — while the user believes they applied three independent criteria. The top-ranked trenches cluster within a few hundred metres of the existing 유적분포 polygons; excavation finds material there; the report presen …
- 제안 수정: Add two sentences to the help at trench_suggestion_dialog.py:452: "<b>가정 명시</b>: '유적 근접' 점수는 기존 유적에 가까울수록 높은 점수를 주므로, <b>유적이 군집한다</b>는 가설과 <b>기존 유적 분포가 조사 이력의 산물</b>이라는 편향을 함께 도입합니다. 이 도구로 배치한 트렌치의 결과를 다시 군집의 근거로 제시하면 순환 논증입니다(AHP_GUIDE.md 8장 참조). 빈 구역을 검증하는 조사라면 이 가중치를 0으로 두십시오. <b>중복 계산 주의</b>: AHP 래스터에 '유적까지 거리' 기준을 넣었다면 같은 변수가 두 번 반영됩니다." Add the same as a tooltip on spinWRef/spinRefRadius, and record "ref_proximity_assumption": "closer_to_known_sites_scores_higher" in the run metadata along …

#### [minor] SCI-06 — Cumulative viewshed silently degrades to a binary union above 20 observer points while the layer name still claims an N-point accumulation

- 위치: `tools/viewshed_dialog.py` :4105 (mode switch); 3704 (writes 255 instead of accumulating); 4159 (layer name)
- 문제: With more than 20 observer points, and with the default state of the '누적값을 개수(1~N)로 표시' checkbox (unchecked) and the weighted checkbox off, the tool stops accumulating entirely and writes a flat 255 wherever any observer sees the cell. The layer is nevertheless named 가시권_누적_N개점 — 누적 meaning cumulative — for all five output modes. The switch is disclosed twice, but weakly: a transient message bar line ('모드: 합집합(Union)') that disappears, and the layer metadata kind="union"/units="mask", which a user has to open the layer properties to see. The layer name is the string that ends up in a figure caption and in the AI 조사요약 bundle, and it says the opposite.
- 실패 시나리오: Twenty-five tomb locations are run as 다중 누적 가시권 at the tool's defaults. len(points)=25 > 20, so is_union_mode is True and every visible cell gets 255. The result is added as '가시권_누적_25개점' and styled by apply_viewshed_style as 가시/비가시. The user exports the figure captioned '25개 고분 누적 가시권' and, in the text, describes the red extent as the area visible from the majority of the tombs. The raster contains no counts at all: a cell seen by one tomb is indistinguishable from a cell seen by all twenty-five. Rerunning the identical selection with 20 sites instead of 25 silently produces a genuine 0-20 co …
- 제안 수정: At viewshed_dialog.py:4155-4159 branch the name on the mode that was actually used: `layer_name = f"가시권_합집합_{len(points)}개점 (누적 아님)"` when is_union_mode, `f"가시권_누적개수_{len(points)}개점"` when is_count_mode, `f"가시권_조합(Bit-flag)_{len(points)}개점"` otherwise. Additionally, make the fallback non-silent: since it triggers at a fixed threshold of 20, either raise the threshold under user control or tick 누적값을 개수로 표시 automatically and say so, rather than changing the quantity computed.

#### [minor] SCI-07 — Local AI report asserts a directional distribution of sites from a top-2 tally, with no threshold and no comparison against uniform

- 위치: `tools/ai_local_summarizer.py` :310-322
- 문제: The sentence asserts a spatial pattern ('주로 … 방향에 분포합니다') from an unconditional top-2 slice of a compass tally over 8 sectors. There is no minimum count, no share threshold and no comparison against what a uniform scatter would produce, so the line is emitted with the same confident wording whether the distribution is strongly anisotropic or as close to uniform as the sample permits. This is generated prose in a tool whose whole purpose is to produce '보고서 초안 문장' that users paste into a 지표조사 보고서, and whose Gemini branch is explicitly instructed at ai_report_dialog.py:740 to avoid 과장/추측 — the local branch applies no such rule to itself. It is also computed from the compass bearing of each site relative to the AOI CENTRE, so for an elongated AOI the bearings themselves are an artefact of the …
- 실패 시나리오: Nine reference sites fall inside the buffer, distributed across the eight compass sectors as N=2, NE=1, E=1, SE=1, S=1, SW=1, W=1, NW=1 — the most uniform arrangement nine sites over eight sectors can take. top_dirs returns [('N',2),('NE',1)] and the report line reads '반경 내 유적은 주로 N(2)·NE(1) 방향에 분포합니다.' The user pastes it into the 주변 유적 현황 section. The stated northerly concentration is three of nine sites where uniform expectation is 2.25, and the same code would print the identical sentence shape if all nine sites were due north. A reviewer plots the nine points and the claimed directional bi …
- 제안 수정: Gate the sentence: emit it only when the top sector's share materially exceeds uniform (e.g. top count >= 3 and top count >= 2x the mean per non-empty sector), and otherwise print the full tally with a neutral verb — '반경 내 유적의 방위 분포: N 2, NE 1, E 1, … (뚜렷한 방향성 없음)'. Append the counts and the sector total to the sentence in either case so the reader can judge it, and note that bearings are measured from the AOI centroid.

#### [minor] SCI-08 — [DUPLICATE of AUDIT_BACKLOG G3 — reported only because still live] GeoChem output panel calls the colour-inverted raster '원본 데이터' and recommends it for MaxEnt, contradicting the dialog's own 역추정 caveat

- 위치: `tools/geochem_polygonize_dialog.py` :710-711 (lblOutHelp); 676-683 (class-raster tooltip); 515-518 (dialog header, which says the opposite); 3011-3036 (units metadata carries the preset's 'ppm')
- 문제: The value raster is produced by inverting a rendered WMS colour ramp through a legend, an operation the header (517), the help HTML (~905) and README all correctly describe as 역추정 and explicitly not 원자료. The output-options panel — the text the user is reading at the exact moment they decide what to keep — calls it 원본 데이터, i.e. original data. The neighbouring tooltip then recommends that same raster as sufficient for MaxEnt and statistics, and the layer metadata stamps units with the preset's 'ppm' (geochem_polygonize_dialog.py:3011, PRESETS at 206-210). Together these present an image-inversion, subject to antialiasing, JPEG artefacts, boundary linework and the 고농도 스냅 clamp, as a measured concentration dataset. This is already logged as backlog G3 and the file is outside the current 19-fil …
- 실패 시나리오: A user builds a Pb value raster from the KIGAM geochemical WMS, reads '원본 데이터' on the output panel, leaves the class raster off as the tooltip advises, and enables 구역 통계 over 유적 polygons. The zonal table reports val_mean = 34 for one site, and the layer metadata says units=ppm. The report states '유적 A의 평균 Pb 농도는 34 ppm으로 주변보다 높다' and the figure is offered as evidence of metallurgical activity. The number is the mean of a colour-ramp inversion of a rendered map tile, not a measurement, and if 고농도 스냅 was on it additionally contains pixels forced to the legend maximum.
- 제안 수정: Replace the lblOutHelp text at line 710 with the dialog header's own wording — 'TIP: value 래스터는 WMS 렌더링 색상에서 역추정한 <b>추정값</b>입니다(원자료 측정값이 아님).' — and qualify the MaxEnt/statistics recommendation at 682 by naming the error sources (안티앨리어싱·경계선·압축·스냅 보정). Consider recording units as 'ppm_estimated' at 3011 so downstream tools and the AI summary cannot present it as a measurement.
